\\\"\\\"\\\"读取建议：以规范事件流重建真源，投影漂移或滞后时自愈。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 365→        canonical = self._canonical_state(proposal_id)\\n 366→        rebuilt = self._rebuild_from_events(proposal_id, canonical)\\n 367→        if rebuilt is not None:\\n 368→            stored = self._read_projection(proposal_id)\\n 369→            if stored is None or stored != rebuilt:\\n 370→                self._save(rebuilt)\\n 371→            return rebuilt\\n 372→        proposal = self._read_projection(proposal_id)\\n 373→        if proposal is None:\\n 374→            raise CorrectionNotFoundError(f\\\"修订建议不存在：{proposal_id}\\\")\\n 375→        if proposal.state != canonical:\\n 376→            repaired = proposal.model_copy(update={\\\"state\\\": canonical})\\n 377→            self._save(repaired)\\n 378→            return repaired\\n 379→        return proposal\\n 380→\\n 381→    def _read_projection(self, proposal_id: str) -> CorrectionProposal | None:\\n 382→        with open_database(self.database_path) as database:\\n 383→            row = database.execute(\\n 384→                \\\"SELECT proposal_json FROM correction_proposals WHERE proposal_id = ?\\\",\\n 385→                (proposal_id,),\\n 386→            ).fetchone()\\n 387→        if row is None:\\n 388→            return None\\n 389→        return CorrectionProposal.model_validate(json.loads(str(row[0])))\\n 390→\\n 391→    def _rebuild_from_events(\\n 392→        self, proposal_id: str, canonical_state: str\\n 393→    ) -> CorrectionProposal | None:\\n 394→        \\\"\\\"\\\"从追加事件重建建议合同：事件是真源，投影只是当前快照。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 395→        base: dict[str, Any] | None = None\\n 396→        source_records: list[CorrectionSourceRecord] = []\\n 397→        validations: list[CorrectionValidationRecord] = []\\n 398→        owner_decision: CorrectionOwnerDecision | None = None\\n 399→        publish_key: str | None = None\\n 400→        published_version: CorrectionPublishedVersion | None = None\\n 401→        conflicts: tuple[str, ...] = ()\\n 402→        impact_set: tuple[str, ...] = ()\\n 403→        for event in self.event_store.read_all():\\n 404→            payload = event.payload\\n 405→            if event.event_type == \\\"correction.proposal.submitted\\\":\\n 406→                candidate = payload.get(\\\"proposal\\\")\\n 407→                if isinstance(candidate, dict) and candidate.get(\\\"proposal_id\\\") == proposal_id:\\n 408→                    base = candidate\\n 409→            elif event.event_type == \\\"correction.evidence.appended\\\":\\n 410→                if payload.get(\\\"proposal_id\\\") != proposal_id:\\n 411→                    continue\\n 412→                record = payload.get(\\\"evidence_record\\\")\\n 413→                if isinstance(record, dict):\\n 414→                    source_records.append(CorrectionSourceRecord.model_validate(record))\\n 415→            elif event.event_type == \\\"graph.transition.accepted\\\":\\n 416→                if (\\n 417→                    payload.get(\\\"family\\\") != \\\"revision_approval\\\"\\n 418→                    or payload.get(\\\"object_id\\\") != proposal_id\\n 419→                ):\\n 420→                    continue\\n 421→                guard_evidence = payload.get(\\\"guard_evidence\\\")\\n 422→                if not isinstance(guard_evidence, dict):\\n 423→                    continue\\n 424→                validation = guard_evidence.get(\\\"validation_record\\\")\\n 425→                if isinstance(validation, dict):\\n 426→                    record = CorrectionValidationRecord.model_validate(validation)\\n 427→                    validations = [\\n 428→                        item for item in validations if item.validation_id != record.validation_id\\n 429→                    ] + [record]\\n 430→                    conflicts = tuple(record.conflicts) or conflicts\\n 431→                    impact_set = tuple(record.impact_set) or impact_set\\n 432→                decision = guard_evidence.get(\\\"decision_record\\\")\\n 433→                if isinstance(decision, dict):\\n 434→                    owner_decision = CorrectionOwnerDecision.model_validate(decision)\\n 435→                    if owner_decision.decision == \\\"approve\\\" and owner_decision.approval_id:\\n 436→                        publish_key = f\\\"correction.publish:{owner_decision.approval_id}\\\"\\n 437→            elif event.event_type == \\\"correction.revision.published\\\":\\n 438→                if payload.get(\\\"proposal_id\\\") != proposal_id:\\n 439→                    continue\\n 440→                version = payload.get(\\\"published_version\\\")\\n 441→                if isinstance(version, dict):\\n 442→                    published_version = CorrectionPublishedVersion.model_validate(version)\\n 443→        if base is None:\\n 444→            return None\\n 445→        fields = dict(base)\\n 446→        fields.update(\\n 447→            {\\n 448→                \\\"source_records\\\": tuple(source_records),\\n 449→                \\\"validations\\\": tuple(validations),\\n 450→                \\\"owner_decision\\\": owner_decision,\\n 451→                \\\"conflicts\\\": conflicts,\\n 452→                \\\"impact_set\\\": impact_set,\\n 453→                \\\"publish_idempotency_key\\\": publish_key,\\n 454→                \\\"published_version\\\": published_version,\\n 455→                \\\"state\\\": canonical_state,\\n 456→            }\\n 457→        )\\n 458→        return CorrectionProposal.model_validate(fields)\\n 459→\\n 460→    def _canonical_state(self, proposal_id: str) -> str:\\n 461→        state = self.executor.state()\\n 462→        current = state.get(\\\"revision_approval\\\", {}).get(proposal_id)\\n 463→        if current is None:\\n 464→            return RevisionApprovalState.SUBMITTED.value\\n 465→        return str(current)\\n 466→\\n 467→    def _save(self, proposal: CorrectionProposal) -> None:\\n 468→        payload = proposal.model_dump(mode=\\\"json\\\")\\n 469→        with open_database(self.database_path) as database:\\n 470→            database.execute(\\n 471→                \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 472→                INSERT INTO correction_proposals (\\n 473→                    proposal_id, project_id, state, proposal_json, created_at\\n 474→                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\\n 475→                ON CONFLICT(proposal_id) DO UPDATE\\n 476→                SET state = excluded.state, proposal_json = excluded.proposal_json\\n 477→                \\\"\\\"\\\",\\n 478→                (\\n 479→                    proposal.proposal_id,\\n 480→                    proposal.project_id,\\n 481→                    proposal.state,\\n 482→                    _canonical_json_text(payload),\\n 483→                    proposal.submitted_at.isoformat(),\\n 484→                ),\\n 485→            )\\n 486→\\n 487→    def _load_or_none(self, proposal_id: str) -> CorrectionProposal | None:\\n 488→        canonical = self._canonical_state(proposal_id)\\n 489→        rebuilt = self._rebuild_from_events(proposal_id, canonical)\\n 490→        if rebuilt is not None:\\n 491→            return rebuilt\\n 492→        return self._read_projection(proposal_id)\\n 493→\\n 494→    # ── 幂等账本 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n 495→\\n 496→    def _claim(self, *, key: str, operation: str, result: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\\n 497→        \\\"\\\"\\\"登记操作结果；返回 True 表示首次登记。同键同结果视为重放。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 498→        result_digest = _payload_digest(result)\\n 499→        with open_database(self.database_path) as database:\\n 500→            row = database.execute(\\n 501→                \\\"SELECT operation, result_digest FROM idempotency_keys WHERE idempotency_key = ?\\\",\\n 502→                (key,),\\n 503→            ).fetchone()\\n 504→            if row is not None:\\n 505→                if str(row[0]) != operation or str(row[1]) != result_digest:\\n 506→                    raise CorrectionConflictError(f\\\"同一幂等键对应了不同操作或结果：{key}\\\")\\n 507→                return False\\n 508→            database.execute(\\n 509→                \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 510→                INSERT INTO idempotency_keys (\\n 511→                    idempotency_key, operation, result_digest, created_at\\n 512→                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\\n 513→                \\\"\\\"\\\",\\n 514→                (key, operation, result_digest, datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat()),\\n 515→            )\\n 516→        return True\\n 517→\\n 518→    def _recorded_result_digest(self, key: str) -> str | None:\\n 519→        with open_database(self.database_path) as database:\\n 520→            row = database.execute(\\n 521→                \\\"SELECT result_digest FROM idempotency_keys WHERE idempotency_key = ?\\\",\\n 522→                (key,),\\n 523→            ).fetchone()\\n 524→        return None if row is None else str(row[0])\\n 525→\\n 526→    # ── 事件与迁移 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n 527→\\n 528→    def _append_business_event(\\n 529→        self,\\n 530→        *,\\n 531→        event_type: str,\\n 532→        event_kind: str,\\n 533→        proposal_id: str,\\n 534→        project_id: str,\\n 535→        actor_id: str,\\n 536→        occurred_at: datetime,\\n 537→        payload: dict[str, Any],\\n 538→        idempotency_key: str,\\n 539→    ) -> None:\\n 540→        event = WorkflowEvent(\\n 541→            schema_version=\\\"1.0\\\",\\n 542→            event_id=stable_id(\\n 543→                event_kind, project_id, _RUN_ID, proposal_id, _payload_digest(payload)\\n 544→            ),\\n 545→            project_id=project_id,\\n 546→            run_id=_RUN_ID,\\n 547→            event_type=event_type,\\n 548→            occurred_at=occurred_at,\\n 549→            actor_id=actor_id,\\n 550→            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,\\n 551→            payload=payload,\\n 552→        )\\n 553→        self.event_store.append(event)\\n 554→\\n 555→    def _transition(\\n 556→        self,\\n 557→        *,\\n 558→        proposal: CorrectionProposal,\\n 559→        to_state: RevisionApprovalState,\\n 560→        request_id: str,\\n 561→        evidence: dict[str, Any],\\n 562→        actor_id: str,\\n 563→        occurred_at: datetime,\\n 564→    ) -> None:\\n 565→        try:\\n 566→            edge = binding_for_transition(proposal.state, to_state.value)\\n 567→        except ValueError as error:\\n 568→            raise CorrectionStateError(str(error)) from error\\n 569→        request = TransitionRequest(\\n 570→            schema_version=\\\"1.0\\\",\\n 571→            request_id=request_id,\\n 572→            project_id=proposal.project_id,\\n 573→            run_id=_RUN_ID,\\n 574→            family=\\\"revision_approval\\\",\\n 575→            object_id=proposal.proposal_id,\\n 576→            from_state=proposal.state,\\n 577→            to_state=to_state.value,\\n 578→            trigger=edge.trigger,\\n 579→            evidence={\\\"target_object_id\\\": proposal.proposal_id, **evidence},\\n 580→            actor_id=actor_id,\\n 581→            occurred_at=occurred_at,\\n 582→        )\\n 583→        try:\\n 584→            stored = self.executor.submit(request)\\n 585→        except EventConflictError as error:\\n 586→            raise CorrectionConflictError(str(error)) from error\\n 587→        if stored.event_type == \\\"graph.transition.rejected\\\":\\n 588→            reason = str(stored.payload.get(\\\"reason\\\", \\\"unknown\\\"))\\n 589→            guard_reason = stored.payload.get(\\\"guard_reason\\\")\\n 590→            detail = f\\\"{reason}:{guard_reason}\\\" if guard_reason else reason\\n 591→            raise CorrectionStateError(f\\\"修订状态迁移被拒绝：{detail}\\\")\\n 592→\\n 593→    # ── 快照绑定核验 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n 594→\\n 595→    def _snapshot_path(self, report: str, snapshot_id: str) -> Path:\\n 596→        return self.project_root / \\\"snapshots\\\" / \\\"reports\\\" / report / f\\\"{snapshot_id}.json\\\"\\n 597→\\n 598→    def _verify_target_snapshot(\\n 599→        self,\\n 600→        *,\\n 601→        project_id: str,\\n 602→        report: _REPORT,\\n 603→        snapshot_id: str,\\n 604→        snapshot_sha256: str,\\n 605→    ) -> ReportSnapshotManifest:\\n 606→        path = self._snapshot_path(report, snapshot_id)\\n 607→        if not path.is_file():\\n 608→            raise CorrectionBindingError(\\n 609→                \\\"修订包绑定的报告快照在项目中不存在，请基于最新报告重新导出修订包。\\\"\\n 610→            )\\n 611→        raw = path.read_bytes()\\n 612→        if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != snapshot_sha256:\\n 613→            raise CorrectionBindingError(\\\"修订包绑定的快照摘要与项目快照不一致，快照可能已被取代。\\\")\\n 614→        try:\\n 615→            manifest = ReportSnapshotManifest.model_validate(json.loads(raw))\\n 616→            recomputed = compute_locked_snapshot(\\n 617→                kind=\\\"report\\\", report=report, manifest=manifest.model_dump(mode=\\\"json\\\")\\n 618→            )\\n 619→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, SnapshotIntegrityError, ValueError) as error:\\n 620→            raise CorrectionBindingError(\\\"修订绑定的快照不是有效的报告快照。\\\") from error\\n 621→        if manifest.project_id != project_id:\\n 622→            raise CorrectionBindingError(\\\"修订包与当前项目不匹配。\\\")\\n 623→        if manifest.report != report:\\n 624→            raise CorrectionBindingError(\\\"修订包声明的报告类型与快照不一致。\\\")\\n 625→        if recomputed.snapshot_id != snapshot_id:\\n 626→            raise CorrectionBindingError(\\\"修订绑定的快照标识与内容身份不一致。\\\")\\n 627→        latest = self._latest_snapshot_created_at(report)\\n 628→        if latest is not None and manifest.created_at < latest:\\n 629→            raise CorrectionBindingError(\\n 630→                \\\"该报告已存在更新的版本，请基于最新报告重新导出修订包后再提交。\\\"\\n 631→            )\\n 632→        return manifest\\n 633→\\n 634→    def _latest_snapshot_created_at(self, report: str) -> datetime | None:\\n 635→        \\\"\\\"\\\"只以已登记版本判断当前快照，忽略发布失败留下的候选文件。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 636→        with open_database(self.database_path) as database:\\n 637→            row = database.execute(\\n 638→                \\\"SELECT MAX(created_at) FROM report_snapshots WHERE report_kind = ?\\\",\\n 639→                (report,),\\n 640→            ).fetchone()\\n 641→        if row is None or row[0] is None:\\n 642→            return None\\n 643→        return _offset_datetime(datetime.fromisoformat(str(row[0])))\\n 644→\\n 645→    # ── 操作 1：submit ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n 646→\\n 647→    def submit(\\n 648→        self,\\n 649→        *,\\n 650→        project_id: str,\\n 651→        report: _REPORT,\\n 652→        snapshot_id: str,\\n 653→        snapshot_sha256: str,\\n 654→        target_type: _TARGET_TYPE,\\n 655→        target_id: str,\\n 656→        current_visible_value: str,\\n 657→        rationale_zh: str,\\n 658→        submitted_by: str,\\n 659→        report_owner_id: str,\\n 660→        proposed_value: report_owner_id=report_owner_id,\\n 711→            attachments=attachments,\\n 712→            state=RevisionApprovalState.SUBMITTED.value,\\n 713→        )\\n 714→        self._append_business_event(\\n 715→            event_type=\\\"correction.proposal.submitted\\\",\\n 716→            event_kind=\\\"correction-submitted\\\",\\n 717→ 2：add_evidence ───────────────────────────────────────────────\\n 731→\\n 732→    def add_evidence(\\n 733→        self,\\n 734→        proposal_id: str,\\n 735→        *,\\n 736→        note_zh: \\\"\\\"\\\"向同一建议追加来源；不覆盖原提交，需补证据的建议回到已提交。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 742→        proposal = self.load(proposal_id)\\n 743→        if proposal.state not in (\\n 744→            RevisionApprovalState.SUBMITTED.value,\\n 745→            RevisionApprovalState.NEEDS_EVIDENCE.value,\\n 746→        ):\\n 747→            raise CorrectionStateError(\\\"当前修订状态不允许追加来源材料。\\\")\\n 748→ 756→                }\\n 757→            ),\\n 758→        )\\n 759→        if any(record.evidence_id == evidence_id for record in proposal.source_records):\\n 760→            return proposal\\n 761→           occurred_at=occurred_at,\\n 775→            payload={\\\"proposal_id\\\": proposal_id, \\\"evidence_record\\\": record.model_dump(mode=\\\"json\\\")},\\n 776→ 3：validate ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n 793→\\n 794→    def validate(\\n 795→        self,\\n 796→        proposal_id: str,\\n 797→        *,\\n 798→        outcome: _OUTCOME,\\n 799→        disposition_reason_zh: str,\\n 800→        validated_by: str,\\n 801→        occurred_at: datetime,\\n 802→        identity_verified: bool = True,\\n 803→ proposal = self.load(proposal_id)\\n 812→        validation_id = stable_id(\\n 813→            \\\"correction-validation\\\",\\n 814→            proposal_id,\\n 815→            _payload_digest(\\n 816→                {\\n 817→                    \\\"outcome\\\": outcome,\\n 818→                    \\\"disposition_reason_zh\\\": disposition_reason_zh,\\n 819→             \\\"conflicts_checked\\\": conflicts_checked,\\n 823→                    \\\"impact_checked\\\": impact_checked,\\n 824→                    \\\"conflicts\\\": list(conflicts),\\n 825→                    \\\"impact_set\\\": list(impact_set),\\n 826→                    \\\"validated_by\\\": validated_by,\\n 827→                }\\n 828→            ),\\n 829→        )\\n 830→        if any(item.validation_id == validation_id for item in proposal.validations):\\n 831→            return proposal\\n 832→        if proposal.state != RevisionApprovalState.SUBMITTED.value:\\n 833→            raise CorrectionStateError(\\\"只有已提交状态的修订建议可以进入验证处置。\\\")\\n 834→        if outcome == \\\"passed\\\" and not all(\\n 835→            (\\n 836→                identity_verified,\\n 837→  )\\n 843→        ):\\n 844→            raise CorrectionStateError(\\n 845→                \\\"来源身份、上下文、证据定位、冲突和影响范围未全部核验，不能提交批准。\\\"\\n 846→            )\\n 847→        record = identity_verified=identity_verified,\\n 852→            context_located=context_located,\\n 853→            locator_verified=locator_verified,\\n 854→            conflicts_checked=conflicts_checked,\\n 855→            impact_checked=impact_checked,\\n 856→            conflicts=conflicts,\\n 857→            impact_set=impact_set,\\n 858→            validated_by=validated_by,\\n 859→            validated_at=occurred_at,\\n 860→        )\\n 861→        outcome_state = {\\n 862→            \\\"needs_evidence\\\": OK.\\n\\n**Issue K: schema contract vs service: `publish_idempotency_key`** — In the service, `publish_idempotency_key` is set at approval time (`correction.publish:{approval_id}`). The schema requires publish_idempotency_key for approved/published states. Good. But the schema also requires `owner_decision.decision == \\\"approve\\\"` for approved AND published states. Published requires both. Fine.\\n\\n**Issue L: Schema `InternalLabelGuard` coverage**: For `target_label_zh` (ProseZh) — the guard rejects labels equal to internal words like \\\"claim\\\". A target label \\\"claim 对比\\\" would... not exact match, fine. But a user's rationale might legitimately contain \\\"事实\\\" — not in the list. The word list is exact-match, so no false positives except weird cases. But what if a user's rationale says \\\"我批准该修订\\\" containing \\\"approve\\\"? Chinese \\\"批准\\\" not \\\"approve\\\". The list is English internal labels. OK. But wait — ProseZh is applied to user_reason_zh, rationale, disposition_reason_zh, etc. A Chinese sentence containing the English word \\\"file\\\" (e.g., \\\"请查看 file 附件\\\")? Unlikely in a Chinese report context. Edge case, low severity. Actually \\\"file\\\" is in the list, \\\"link\\\" too — a Chinese user could write \\\"链接\\\" not \\\"link\\\". Acceptable.\\n\\n**Issue M: The schema requires `proposed_value` OR `proposed_explanation_zh`** via root anyOf — fine. But service requires `_is_nonblank_str(proposed_value) or _is_nonblank_str(proposed_explanation)`.\\n\\n**Issue N: `state` transitions in service vs graph executor.** `_transition` uses `binding_for_transition(proposal.state, to_state.value)`. `binding_for_transition` uses the correction bindings. For add_evidence from needs_evidence → submitted. Good.\\n\\nBut here's a subtle one: In `add_evidence`, when the proposal is in NEEDS_EVIDENCE, after appending evidence it transitions to submitted. The `_transition` is called with `proposal=updated` where updated has the new source_records but state=needs_evidence. from_state = proposal.state. OK.\\n\\n**Issue O: `validate` requires `impact_set` non-empty for passed.** But it doesn't require conflicts to be checked... it does: `conflicts_checked` required True. OK.\\n\\n**Issue P: `record_owner_decision` for `reject`/`needs_evidence`** — creates decision_record with approval_id None; transition to REJECTED/NEEDS_EVIDENCE. Then `_save`. But there's no side effect for reject — fine. Wait, there IS a bug: in the replay path, `if recorded.decision == decision and recorded.approval_id == approval_id` — for reject, approval_id is None for both recorded and new, so exact replay returns early — but the replay early-return happens BEFORE the state check! So after a proposal has been rejected (state=rejected) and owner later... no wait, replay requires the same decision with same approval_id (None) — any reject replay returns early. But then a DIFFERENT decision (e.g., needs_evidence) after a reject: recorded.decision=reject ≠ needs_evidence → falls to state check → state is REJECTED ≠ VALIDATED_PENDING → error. Good, rejected is terminal.\\n\\nBut here's a subtle bug: if the proposal was VALIDATED and owner decided \\\"reject\\\", then... nothing wrong.\\n\\nWhat about the replay path being BEFORE the state/owner checks — an attacker replays an approve? The replay requires recorded.approval_id == approval_id (deterministic from decided_by + reason). Only the original owner's decision has that approval_id. So a replay by a non-owner returns existing proposal — acceptable (it doesn't create new side effects beyond the approval side-effect which is keyed by approval_id and already exists).\\n\\nHmm wait — actually there IS a possible issue: the replay path calls `_record_approval_side_effect` which re-appends the `revision.approve` event. EventStore.append with same event_id → dedupe (returns existing) or conflict? Let me check event_store.append behavior. If same event_id with same payload → returns existing; if different payload → EventConflictError. The side effect payload is deterministic → same → OK.\\n\\n**Issue Q: The `validations` tuple dedupe in `_rebuild_from_events`** — `[item for item in validations if item.validation_id != record.validation_id] + [record]` — this REORDERS by re-adding at the end. But if two validation events with the same validation_id exist (impossible, deterministic id), fine.\\n\\n**Issue R: `_rebuild_from_events` publishes** — only the LAST published event is kept (overwrites published_version). If two published events exist (the crash-window divergence), the last one wins. This means the projection is \\\"last writer wins\\\" — consistent with the bug in Issue I.\\n\\n**Issue S: the `submit` duplicate check** — `_load_or_none(proposal_id)`. proposal_id includes snapshot_sha256. If the same correction is re-submitted against an UPDATED snapshot (v2), proposal_id differs (different snapshot) → new proposal. OK.\\n\\n**Issue T: `_verify_target_snapshot` reads the snapshot from the project `snapshots/reports/{report}/{snapshot_id}.json` path** — the schema's snapshot binding uses the snapshot's content digest; the service reads the file and rehashes. Fine. But the snapshot_id in the DB path is the locked snapshot id. OK.\\n\\n**Issue U: Report kind A/B/C enum in schema, and service uses `_REPORT = Literal[\\\"A\\\",\\\"B\\\",\\\"C\\\"]`. But the actual `ReportKind` enum — let me check the values. If ReportKind values are \\\"A\\\",\\\"B\\\",\\\"C\\\" — ok.\\n\\n**Issue V: The `report_version` binding** — schema allows any string; service sets from manifest and enforces vN format only at publish (`_next_report_version`). If the manifest's report_version is not vN (e.g., \\\"a-v1\\\"), publish raises CorrectionPublishError \\\"当前报告版本无法递增\\\". So submit succeeds but publish becomes permanently impossible. The contract test uses report_version \\\"a-v1\\\" — which would make publish fail. In the service tests, report_version is \\\"v1\\\". So schema-level allows \\\"a-v1\\\" but service can never publish such a proposal — inconsistency between schema and service, but minor (the schema is the export contract; the service enforces version format at publish). Worth flagging as low severity: schema should constrain report_version pattern to `^v\\\\d+$` OR the service should reject at submit. Actually the schema allows `report_version: minLength 1`, and test fixture uses \\\"a-v1\\\" — meaning the exported package could contain a version the system cannot increment. Fail-closed at publish though. Low severity.\\n\\n**Issue W: `prior_proposal_id`** — the schema has it but the service never sets it! The service's CorrectionProposal model has no prior_proposal_id field. Design says \\\"发布幂等键和前后版本关系\\\" — the \\\"前后版本关系\\\" is the published_version.supersedes_snapshot_id. prior_proposal_id is in the schema but never populated by the service. Since schema doesn't require it, fine, but the field is dead weight — no chain of proposals for the same target across versions. Not a defect, but a \\\"追加历史\\\" gap: a user correction to the SAME target in v3 has no link to the earlier v1→v2 correction proposal. Minor.\\n\\n**Issue X: The approval side effect event `revision.approve`** — payload decision is \\\"approve\\\" (lowercase), while test uses \\\"approved\\\". Not relevant to service correctness; the executor's side_effects for revision.approve writes to approvals.jsonl. Let me verify the executor.side_effects handles \\\"revision.approve\\\".\\n\\n**Issue Y: `state_progress_zh` test** — asserts no ASCII letters and no internal state words. The mapping contains \\\"报告负责人\\\" etc. Fine.\\n\\n**Issue Z: schema `snapshot_content_digest` is the sha256 of the whole snapshot FILE?** In the service, snapshot_sha256 = sha256 of file bytes. In the schema, snapshot_content_digest = HexDigest. The compute_locked_snapshot sha256 = sha256 of canonical JSON of the manifest payload. The file bytes ARE the canonical JSON + \\\"\\\\n\\\". So sha256(file bytes) == compute's sha256? compute_locked_snapshot: `encoded = _canonical_json(payload)` which appends \\\"\\\\n\\\"; digest = sha256(encoded). The file is written via `_canonical_json(payload)` too. So file bytes == encoded → sha256 matches. But consider: proposal in NEEDS_EVIDENCE, evidence already recorded (from an interrupted add_evidence that appended the event but crashed before transition?). Sequence in add_evidence: append event → transition (if needs_evidence) → save. If crash after event append, before transition: proposal still in NEEDS_EVIDENCE, but evidence_record already in source_records (rebuilt from events). Retry: `any(record.evidence_id == evidence_id ...)` → True → return proposal with state NEEDS_EVIDENCE — the transition to submitted NEVER happens! The evidence is recorded but the proposal stays in needs_evidence forever (the state machine requires the transition event). The retry short-circuits before the transition. That's a genuine crash-recovery bug: the \\\"recovery\\\" for the needs_evidence→submitted transition is broken by the early return. Let me verify the code path:\\n\\n```python\\nif any(record.evidence_id == evidence_id for record in proposal.source_records):\\n    return proposal\\n```\\n\\nYes — after crash between event append and transition, the retry returns early and never transitions. The proposal remains stuck in needs_evidence with evidence appended. The idempotency ledger doesn't cover add_evidence at all (no _claim call — it relies on the event's idempotency_key). The event itself is idempotent (dedupe), but the TRANSITION side effect is not resumable. This is a medium severity crash-recovery defect. Also for the SUBMITTED case (add evidence while submitted), no transition needed — fine.\\n\\nWait, actually let me double-check the crash window: `_append_business_event` then `self._transition(...)` then `_save`. If crash after append, on retry the early-return triggers → stuck in needs_evidence. Yes. Unless `load()` rebuilds state from the canonical event stream: `_canonical_state` reads executor.state() which comes from the transition events. The transition never happened → state = needs_evidence. So the proposal shows needs_evidence with the new evidence record present, and `add_evidence` on retry returns early (idempotent), so the user can never transition back to submitted. They could... call add_evidence again with DIFFERENT note → new evidence_id → appends another record AND transitions. So a workaround exists (add more evidence), but the exact retry fails to restore. This is a real but low-medium severity issue: idempotent replay of add_evidence doesn't resume the pending transition. Design says \\\"若服务在事件写入后崩溃，重放同一幂等键返回既有结果\\\" — for add_evidence, the \\\"既有结果\\\" is inconsistent (event written but transition not). Actually the design contract is that operations are atomic via idempotency; here the idempotency key for the event is `correction.evidence:{proposal_id}:{evidence_id}` — and the transition uses a different request id. So the transition is not covered by the same idempotency key. The fix: after the early-return detection, check if the state is NEEDS_EVIDENCE and the proposal contains this evidence and no pending transition — perform the transition. Or record the operation in the idempotency ledger and, on replay, resume by re-running the transition deterministically.\\n\\nAlso, similar crash windows exist in `validate` (append event → transition → save; crash after append → retry returns early at `if any(...validation_id...)` → stuck in submitted with validation record present but no transition). Same pattern! And `record_owner_decision` handles this correctly via the replay path (the special re-run of side effect). But note: in `record_owner_decision`, the replay path does NOT re-run the transition, but there's no transition needed after the decision event (the decision event IS the transition — wait, no: `_transition` appends the graph.transition.accepted event; the decision event is the transition evidence embedded in it. The owner decision doesn't append a separate business event — the transition carries `decision_record`. So crash after transition append → retry → replay path re-runs `_record_approval_side_effect` (idempotent) and returns. Good — decision is covered.\\n\\nBut `validate` and `add_evidence` crash windows are NOT covered. The design says \\\"所有操作通过确定性幂等键抵抗检查点重放\\\" — the event append is idempotent, but the transition is a second write that the early return skips on replay. So the claim \\\"重放同一幂等键返回既有结果，不重复批准或发布\\\" holds for approve/publish but not for validate/add_evidence transitions. Since the projection `load()` rebuilds from events, the state after retry shows submitted + validation record — and `validate` on retry returns the proposal (early return) — so the caller sees the validation record but state submitted. The user-facing view would say \\\"已收到修订建议，正在核验\\\" while the validation is recorded. The Agent would then... the orchestrator typically calls validate once. This is a crash-window inconsistency. Medium-low severity, but exactly in scope for \\\"幂等恢复\\\" review.\\n\\nActually — wait. Let me re-check validate's early return:\\n\\n```python\\nif any(item.validation_id == validation_id for item in proposal.validations):\\n    return proposal\\n```\\n\\nOn retry after crash (transition not yet appended): proposal.load() rebuilds validations from events (the validation record was embedded in the transition event as guard_evidence.validation_record — it's NOT in the event until the transition appends!). Hmm — actually, `_rebuild_from_events` reads validation records from `graph.transition.accepted` events' `guard_evidence.validation_record`. If the transition never appended, the validation record is NOT in the event stream — so on retry, proposal.validations is empty → the early return does NOT trigger → the retry proceeds to `if proposal.state != SUBMITTED` → state IS submitted → proceeds → re-appends event? No: `_append_business_event` is only called in submit and add_evidence and publish_failed. In validate, the transition event IS the only event. The validation record is embedded in the transition evidence. So on retry, validate re-runs `_transition` with the same request_id → executor dedupes by idempotency key (the transition request's idempotency_key derives from request_id?) → returns the existing event if accepted, or if the first attempt crashed BEFORE the transition append, the retry appends it now → then `_save` → works!\\n\\nSo for validate, the crash window is self-healing because the early-return detection reads from the event-rebuilt projection, and the validation record only appears in the stream via the transition event itself. Wait but the early return exists to dedupe when the event IS in the stream. If the transition appended but _save crashed: retry → load → validations includes the record (from transition event) → early return → but the state: `_canonical_state` reads from executor state — the transition was accepted → state = outcome state (e.g., validated_pending_user_approval) → load returns that. So early return returns the proposal in the correct state. Good.\\n\\nBUT — for add_evidence: the evidence event `correction.evidence.appended` IS a separate event that contains the record. So the crash window (event appended, transition not) leaves the record in the stream → retry early-returns → transition never happens → STUCK. Confirmed asymmetric: add_evidence is the one with the stuck-state bug. For validate, the record only enters the stream with the transition, so it self-heals.\\n\\nLet me double-check add_evidence crash window ordering in code:\\n\\n```python\\nself._append_business_event(event_type=\\\"correction.evidence.appended\\\", ...)\\nupdated = proposal.model_copy(update={\\\"source_records\\\": (*proposal.source_records, record)})\\nif proposal.state == RevisionApprovalState.NEEDS_EVIDENCE.value:\\n    self._transition(...)\\n    updated = updated.model_copy(update={\\\"state\\\": SUBMITTED})\\nself._save(updated)\\n```\\n\\nYes: crash after `_append_business_event`, before `_transition`. Retry: load → source_records contains the record (rebuilt from event) → `any(record.evidence_id == evidence_id ...)` → True → return proposal (state NEEDS_EVIDENCE, not submitted). STUCK. The proposal can never transition to submitted via retry. Confirmed bug — medium severity (crash window + stuck state; user workaround is to add another evidence note).\\n\\nThe fix: on the early-return path, if state == NEEDS_EVIDENCE, still perform the transition (it's deterministic and idempotent at the executor level via request_id). I.e., move the transition to be attempted even when the record already exists, or better: check \\\"record exists AND state == needs_evidence AND no transition happened\\\" → run transition.\\n\\nAlso for submit: crash after `_append_business_event` (submitted event) before `_save`: retry → `_load_or_none` → rebuilds base from submitted event → returns existing (state submitted). Fine — submit is atomic enough (single event). Good.\\n\\nFor publish: crash windows — the divergent materials issue (Issue I) and the claim ordering.\\n\\nLet me also double check publish's early claim check order vs state: `publish()` reads `existing_digest` from claim BEFORE checking state. OK.\\n\\nOne more thing — Issue I detail: when retry with different materials succeeds (the divergence), `_verify_recorded_publish` on the NEXT retry: existing_digest = digest of second version; proposal.published_version = second → matches. It does NOT compare against the registered manifest_json in the DB. So a snapshot FILE could exist in the project with a valid content-addressed identity but NOT registered in report_snapshots (e.g., a leftover candidate from a failed publish — the design says `_latest_snapshot_created_at` \\\"只以已登记版本判断当前快照，忽略发布失败留下的候选文件\\\"). Consider: publish fails after lock (snapshot file written to disk) but before registration. The candidate file sits in snapshots/reports/A/. Later, a user (or orchestrator) exports a package from that candidate — the file exists, digest matches, project/report match, snapshot identity OK, created_at < latest registered → REJECTED. Good. But if the failed publish's candidate file has created_at >= latest... could it pass the staleness check and be submitted against a NON-registered snapshot? The candidate's manifest created_at is the rebuild time, before the publish attempt, which is before the latest registration time (if any publish succeeded later) → rejected. If NO registration exists for the report... then latest is None → check skipped → a candidate file could be submitted against. But if no registration exists, the report was never locked — a candidate file could only exist from a failed publish, and a failed publish requires an approved proposal which requires a registered snapshot. So in practice, latest is never None after any correction flow. Content-addressing protects. But the DB row's manifest_json could diverge from the file (e.g., manual edit). The service trusts the file. Minor.\\n\\n**Issue DD — mypy/ruff:** can't run. The architecture (frozen six-state machine, guard reuse, append-only events, deterministic IDs, projection self-heal) is sound and well-tested, but I found one blocking functional defect that permanently disables the revision workflow after the first publish, plus two crash-recovery/idempotency defects and several contract-strength gaps. Details below; each item is labeled **缺陷 (D)** or **问题/决策点 (Q)**.\\n\\n### D1 — BLOCKING: 最新快照绑定检查在首次发布后永久拒绝所有新提交\\n\\n`correction_service.py:627-631` (`_verify_target_snapshot`) rejects a proposal when `manifest.created_at < MAX(created_at) FROM report_snapshots WHERE report_kind = ?` (`_latest_snapshot_created_at`, `correction_service.py:634-643`). But `_register_new_version` (`correction_service.py:1258-1261`) registers the new version row with `occurred_at.isoformat()` — the **publish wall-clock time** — not the manifest's `created_at`.\\n\\nConsequence, concretely (numbers from the test fixtures): v1 registered at `2026-08-01T08:00:00+00:00`; v2 manifest `created_at = 2026-08-31T09:00:00+00:00`; publish at `15:00` → v2 row `created_at = 15:00`. Any later `submit` against the **current newest** snapshot (v2, manifest 09:00) computes `09:00 < 15:00` → rejected with \\\"该报告已存在更新的版本，请基于最新报告重新导出修订包后再提交\\\". Re-exporting from the live site does **not** help (the immutable snapshot file carries the same `created_at`). After the first publish, no further corrections can be submitted for that report — acceptance #5 (追加历史) is operationally dead. No test covers submit-after-publish.\\n\\nAdditional fragility: `MAX(created_at)` on TEXT over ISO strings with mixed UTC offsets (project timezone Asia/Shanghai) can mis-order (`\\\"…T15:00:00+08:00\\\" > \\\"…T09:00:00+00:00\\\"` lexically even though 07:00Z < 09:00Z), so even the \\\"latest\\\" selection can be wrong.\\n\\n**Remediation**: compare against the latest *registered* snapshot by identity, not by a cross-clock timestamp comparison. Either (a) read the latest row's `manifest_json` and compare `created_at` against the manifest's own `created_at`, or (b) select the latest registered `snapshot_id` and require the target `snapshot_id` to equal it. Store `manifest.created_at` (not publish `occurred_at`) in the row if the timestamp column is meant to be manifest time. Add an integration test: submit → validate → approve → publish → submit a second proposal against v2 must succeed.\\n\\n### D2 — MEDIUM: 发布崩溃窗口可把两个不同快照登记为同一个下一版本（违反\\\"同一批准只登记一个新版本\\\"）\\n\\n`_register_new_version` order (`correction_service.py:1239-1303`): INSERT row → append `correction.revision.published` event → save projection → `_claim` (last). Crash between event append and `_claim` leaves no ledger entry. On retry **with different materials** (same approval): `publish()` finds no claim (`correction_service.py:1035-1037`), state is already `published` so the transition is skipped (`1054`), `_verify_publish_materials` locks a *different* snapshot (different content → different id), and `_register_new_version` INSERTs a **second row with the same `report_version v2`** (`1241-1266` only guards same-snapshot_id), appends a second published event, and the projection/claim now hold the second version. Result: two competing v2 registrations, two published events, orphaned first version, no detection mechanism. `_rebuild_from_events` is last-writer-wins (`correction_service.py:437-442`), so the divergence becomes the accepted truth.\\n\\n**Remediation**: reserve/claim the idempotency key **before** any registration side effect; or when `state == PUBLISHED` and `published_version` exists, fail closed unless the incoming materials' locked snapshot_id equals the already-published `published_version.snapshot_id`. Add a test simulating \\\"published event exists, claim missing, retry with different materials\\\".\\n\\n### D3 — MEDIUM: `add_evidence` 崩溃重放后卡在 `needs_evidence`（过渡不恢复）\\n\\nOrder in `add_evidence` (`correction_service.py:768-789`): append `correction.evidence.appended` event → `_transition` needs_evidence→submitted → save. The dedupe early-return (`759-760`) triggers as soon as the evidence record is in the event stream, **before** the transition. Crash between event append and transition → retry: `load()` rebuilds the record from the stream, early-returns, and the proposal stays in `needs_evidence` forever; the deterministic transition is never resumed. Note the asymmetry: `validate` does **not** have this bug — its record only enters the stream via the transition event itself, so a retry re-runs the transition (self-heals). `record_owner_decision` also handles its crash window correctly (replay re-runs the approval side effect, `920-930`). Only `add_evidence` is broken.\\n\\n**Remediation**: on the early-return path, if `state == NEEDS_EVIDENCE`, still attempt the deterministic `_transition` (idempotent at the executor level via request_id), or reorder so the transition is attempted before/independent of the dedupe return. Add a crash-window test (evidence event present, transition missing → retry must reach `submitted`).\\n\\n### Q1 — 自包含修订包没有生产者，服务模型与导出 Schema 词汇不对应（验收 6 只测了 Schema 层）\\n\\n`src/ci_workflow` contains **no export/producer** for the self-contained package (grep for 修订包/export confirms only error strings and the schema description). The service model and the schema do not map 1:1 anywhere: `report` vs `report_kind`; `snapshot_sha256` vs `snapshot_content_digest`; `rationale_zh` vs `user_reason_zh`; `source_records`/`CorrectionAttachment(label,url)` vs `evidence_items`/`EvidenceItem(evidence_id, source_url|source_version_id, description_zh)`; `conflicts: tuple[str]` vs `Conflict` objects; `impact_set: tuple[str]` vs structured `ImpactSet`; validation `outcome: passed` vs schema `disposition: validated_pending_user_approval`; `published_version` vs `published_report_version_id`. Acceptance #6 is therefore verified only by hand-built contract-test payloads. Bounded question for Codex: is the export adapter deliberately deferred to Phase 9.4 (site-side), with #6 satisfied by schema + contract tests? If 9.1 must ship a producer, that is missing scope.\\n\\n### Q2 — \\\"稳定目标绑定\\\"目前只是格式级绑定\\n\\n`submit` validates `target_id` format (regex, `correction_service.py:296-301`) and snapshot identity/digest/project/report/latestness, but never checks that a `claim` target exists in `manifest.claim_ids`, and `current_visible_value` is never compared with anything in the snapshot (manifests carry ids, not values — so it cannot be). The `validate` step's `identity_verified`/`context_located`/`locator_verified` are **caller-attested booleans** with zero service-side verification (`correction_service.py:834-846` only checks they are all True + impact_set non-empty). For a fail-closed clinical product, consider at minimum checking claim targets against `manifest.claim_ids` at submit, and documenting that value-level verification is delegated to the Agent attestation. Decision point for Codex.\\n\\n### Q3 — 独立质控与重建回执是不透明的自证，未与真实产物绑定\\n\\n`_verify_publish_materials` format-checks `rebuilt_artifacts` (`artifact_id` + arbitrary `sha256`, `1152-1158`) and `independent_qc` (`qc_verdict_id`/`reviewer_id`/`accepted`, `1159-1167`; reviewer≠owner enforced at `1166`) but binds nothing to real project artifacts — unlike the snapshot precondition which does full content-address verification (`1170-1201`). Contrast with the existing `g_report_scientific_qc_snapshot_locked` guard (`guards.py:264-298`) which requires `qc_verdict_digest`/`qc_review_input_digest`/object binding. Here the QC verdict has no digest bound to the new snapshot. Consider binding the QC verdict digest and rebuilt-artifact file digests the same way `_verify_target_snapshot` binds the snapshot.\\n\\n### Q4 — 首次提交者决定报告所有者（first-writer-wins）\\n\\n`proposal_id` (`correction_service.py:666-682`) excludes `submitted_by`, `attachments`, and `report_owner_id`. Two identical corrections from different users yield the same proposal_id; the second submit silently returns the first proposal, and if the true report owner was the second submitter, they cannot approve (`933` `decided_by != proposal.report_owner_id`). Dedup is reasonable, but owner binding should arguably be a property of the report, not the first submitter. Decision point for Codex.\\n\\n### Q5 (LOW) — 杂项\\n\\n- `_verify_publish_materials` reads the old snapshot file at `1186-1192` without wrapping `FileNotFoundError`; a missing old snapshot file propagates raw and **bypasses `_record_publish_failure`**, violating the design promise \\\"失败保留批准状态并追加失败原因事件\\\".\\n- `qc_checked_at` is only non-blank-checked (`1160-1164`); the schema enforces `date-time` format — service check weaker.\\n- Schema allows `new_snapshot_id == snapshot_id` and an empty `impact_set` in `published` state (no cross-field constraints); the service enforces both, so the machine contract is weaker than the runtime — acceptable but should be documented or tightened (`schema.json:339-421`).\\n- Schema allows `report_version: \\\"a-v1\\\"` (any non-blank string; contract test fixture uses `a-v1`), while the service can only ever publish `vN` (`_next_report_version`). A package with a non-`vN` version passes the schema but can never be published — fail-closed at publish, but the schema should constrain the pattern.\\n\\n## Evidence And Assumptions\\n\\n- Evidence: direct reads of every file listed above; line references included per finding. The service model and the schema do not map 1:1 anywhere: `report` vs `report_kind`; `snapshot_sha256` vs `snapshot_content_digest`; `rationale_zh` vs `user_reason_zh`; `source_records`/`CorrectionAttachment(label,url)` vs `evidence_items`/`EvidenceItem(evidence_id, source_url|source_version_id, description_zh)`; `conflicts: tuple[str]` vs `Conflict` objects; `impact_set: tuple[str]` vs structured `ImpactSet`; validation `outcome: passed` vs schema `disposition: validated_pending_user_approval`; `published_version` vs `published_report_version_id`. Acceptance #6 is therefore verified only by hand-built contract-test payloads. Bounded question for Codex: is the export adapter deliberately deferred to Phase 9.4 (site-side), with #6 satisfied by schema + contract tests? If 9.1 must ship a producer, that is missing scope.\\n\\n### Q2 — \\\"稳定目标绑定\\\"目前只是格式级绑定\\n\\n`submit` validates `target_id` format (regex, `correction_service.py:296-301`) and snapshot identity/digest/project/report/latestness, but never checks that a `claim` target exists in `manifest.claim_ids`, and `current_visible_value` is never compared with anything in the snapshot (manifests carry ids, not values — so it cannot be). The `validate` step's `identity_verified`/`context_located`/`locator_verified` are **caller-attested booleans** with zero service-side verification (`correction_service.py:834-846` only checks they are all True + impact_set non-empty). For a fail-closed clinical product, consider at minimum checking claim targets against `manifest.claim_ids` at submit, and documenting that value-level verification is delegated to the Agent attestation. Decision point for Codex.\\n\\n### Q3 — 独立质控与重建回执是不透明的自证，未与真实产物绑定\\n\\n`_verify_publish_materials` format-checks `rebuilt_artifacts` (`artifact_id` + arbitrary `sha256`, `1152-1158`) and `independent_qc` (`qc_verdict_id`/`reviewer_id`/`accepted`, `1159-1167`; reviewer≠owner enforced at `1166`) but binds nothing to real project artifacts — unlike the snapshot precondition which does full content-address verification (`1170-1201`). Contrast with the existing `g_report_scientific_qc_snapshot_locked` guard (`guards.py:264-298`) which requires `qc_verdict_digest`/`qc_review_input_digest`/object binding. Here the QC verdict has no digest bound to the new snapshot. Consider binding the QC verdict digest and rebuilt-artifact file digests the same way `_verify_target_snapshot` binds the snapshot.\\n\\n### Q4 — 首次提交者决定报告所有者（first-writer-wins）\\n\\n`proposal_id` (`correction_service.py:666-682`) excludes `submitted_by`, `attachments`, and `report_owner_id`. Two identical corrections from different users yield the same proposal_id; the second submit silently returns the first proposal, and if the true report owner was the second submitter, they cannot approve (`933` `decided_by != proposal.report_owner_id`). Dedup is reasonable, but owner binding should arguably be a property of the report, not the first submitter. Decision point for Codex.\\n\\n### Q5 (LOW) — 杂项\\n\\n- `_verify_publish_materials` reads the old snapshot file at `1186-1192` without wrapping `FileNotFoundError`; a missing old snapshot file propagates raw and **bypasses `_record_publish_failure`**, violating the design promise \\\"失败保留批准状态并追加失败原因事件\\\".\\n- `qc_checked_at` is only non-blank-checked (`1160-1164`); the schema enforces `date-time` format — service check weaker.\\n- Schema allows `new_snapshot_id == snapshot_id` and an empty `impact_set` in `published` state (no cross-field constraints); the service enforces both, so the machine contract is weaker than the runtime — acceptable but should be documented or tightened (`schema.json:339-421`).\\n- Schema allows `report_version: \\\"a-v1\\\"` (any non-blank string; contract test fixture uses `a-v1`), while the service can only ever publish `vN` (`_next_report_version`). A package with a non-`vN` version passes the schema but can never be published — fail-closed at publish, but the schema should constrain the pattern.\\n\\n## Evidence And Assumptions\\n\\n- Evidence: direct reads of every file listed above; line references included per finding. GT05 fixture confirmed to contain exactly the 8 revision edges that `correction.py` binds (`tests/graph/test_transition_matrix.py:750-807`), and `transitions.py:340-386` registers the same 8 edges; bindings are validated at import time against the frozen registry (`correction.py:148-180`), and the test asserts binding==fixture (test_correction_flow.py:158-193). Schema state enum == `RevisionApprovalState` six values (enums.py:97-103); migration CHECK constraint matches (`0005:4-7`). Event store dedupes by event_id and idempotency fingerprint (`event_store.py:204-238`). Executor short-circuits exact replays by request_id (`executor.py:509-514`). Approval side effect keyed by `correction.approve:{approval_id}` and deduped in the side-effect ledger.\\n- Inference vs evidence: D1, D2, D3, Q1–Q5 are **static-analysis conclusions**; D1 and D3 rest on execution-order reasoning over the exact code paths cited. I could not run a live reproduction (Bash denied). Confidence: D1 high (deterministic ordering of timestamps; the only way to avoid it is a code path I did not find — none re-stamps `report_snapshots.created_at` other than `_register_new_version` and the initial `INSERT OR IGNORE` in `source_research_service.py:1940-1954`, which uses the manifest's own timestamp and confirms the invariant the correction publish path breaks). D3 high (early-return precedes transition; verified in code). D2 high (claim is the last write; divergence requires a retry with different materials, which the API accepts — `publish()` takes materials as arguments with no binding to a prior attempt).\\n- Assumptions: the publish orchestration may legitimately re-call `publish` with regenerated materials after an interruption (design.md explicitly says \\\"发布中断后同一批准可恢复\\\"), which is exactly what makes D2 reachable.\\n\\n## Risks, Gaps, And Verification Needs\\n\\n- **未验证**: 测试套件、mypy、Ruff 均未执行（Bash 权限被拒）。实现者自测不作为验收证据（会议上下文亦如此声明）。Codex 需运行：`uv run pytest tests/contract/test_correction_proposal_contract.py tests/integration/test_correction_flow.py tests/integration/test_correction_service.py -q` 及回归（revision/graph/migration/manifest 相关）、`uv run mypy src`、`uv run ruff check`。\\n- **验证需求（按优先级）**:\\n  1. 复现 D1：publish 后对新版本 submit 必须成功（当前预期失败）。\\n  2. 复现 D3：手工删掉 add_evidence 的 transition 事件后重试，状态必须回到 `submitted`。\\n  3. 复现 D2：删除 publish claim 后以不同材料重试，必须失败关闭。\\n  4. 快照跨时区比较（Q5/D1 附带）——混合 `+08:00`/`+00:00` 时 MAX(created_at) 的选择正确性。\\n- **风险**: 若 D1 属实而未被发现，9.1 会在\\\"一次发布后修订流程永久失效\\\"的状态下验收；这是生产阻断级。\\n- 工作树 dirty 573 paths —— 与本次审查无关，但 Codex 提交前应确认只包含本任务改动。\\n\\n## Recommended Next Step\\n\\n1. The architecture (frozen six-state machine, guard reuse, append-only events, deterministic IDs, projection self-heal) is sound and well-tested, but I found one blocking functional defect that permanently disables the revision workflow after the first publish, plus two crash-recovery/idempotency defects and several contract-strength gaps. Details below; each item is labeled **缺陷 (D)** or **问题/决策点 (Q)**.\n\n### D1 — BLOCKING: 最新快照绑定检查在首次发布后永久拒绝所有新提交\n\n`correction_service.py:627-631` (`_verify_target_snapshot`) rejects a proposal when `manifest.created_at < MAX(created_at) FROM report_snapshots WHERE report_kind = ?` (`_latest_snapshot_created_at`, `correction_service.py:634-643`). But `_register_new_version` (`correction_service.py:1258-1261`) registers the new version row with `occurred_at.isoformat()` — the **publish wall-clock time** — not the manifest's `created_at`.\n\nConsequence, concretely (numbers from the test fixtures): v1 registered at `2026-08-01T08:00:00+00:00`; v2 manifest `created_at = 2026-08-31T09:00:00+00:00`; publish at `15:00` → v2 row `created_at = 15:00`. Any later `submit` against the **current newest** snapshot (v2, manifest 09:00) computes `09:00 < 15:00` → rejected with \"该报告已存在更新的版本，请基于最新报告重新导出修订包后再提交\". Re-exporting from the live site does **not** help (the immutable snapshot file carries the same `created_at`). After the first publish, no further corrections can be submitted for that report — acceptance #5 (追加历史) is operationally dead. No test covers submit-after-publish.\n\nAdditional fragility: `MAX(created_at)` on TEXT over ISO strings with mixed UTC offsets (project timezone Asia/Shanghai) can mis-order (`\"…T15:00:00+08:00\" > \"…T09:00:00+00:00\"` lexically even though 07:00Z < 09:00Z), so even the \"latest\" selection can be wrong.\n\n**Remediation**: compare against the latest *registered* snapshot by identity, not by a cross-clock timestamp comparison. Either (a) read the latest row's `manifest_json` and compare `created_at` against the manifest's own `created_at`, or (b) select the latest registered `snapshot_id` and require the target `snapshot_id` to equal it. Store `manifest.created_at` (not publish `occurred_at`) in the row if the timestamp column is meant to be manifest time. Add an integration test: submit → validate → approve → publish → submit a second proposal against v2 must succeed.\n\n### D2 — MEDIUM: 发布崩溃窗口可把两个不同快照登记为同一个下一版本（违反\"同一批准只登记一个新版本\"）\n\n`_register_new_version` order (`correction_service.py:1239-1303`): INSERT row → append `correction.revision.published` event → save projection → `_claim` (last). Crash between event append and `_claim` leaves no ledger entry. On retry **with different materials** (same approval): `publish()` finds no claim (`correction_service.py:1035-1037`), state is already `published` so the transition is skipped (`1054`), `_verify_publish_materials` locks a *different* snapshot (different content → different id), and `_register_new_version` INSERTs a **second row with the same `report_version v2`** (`1241-1266` only guards same-snapshot_id), appends a second published event, and the projection/claim now hold the second version. Result: two competing v2 registrations, two published events, orphaned first version, no detection mechanism. `_rebuild_from_events` is last-writer-wins (`correction_service.py:437-442`), so the divergence becomes the accepted truth.\n\n**Remediation**: reserve/claim the idempotency key **before** any registration side effect; or when `state == PUBLISHED` and `published_version` exists, fail closed unless the incoming materials' locked snapshot_id equals the already-published `published_version.snapshot_id`. Add a test simulating \"published event exists, claim missing, retry with different materials\".\n\n### D3 — MEDIUM: `add_evidence` 崩溃重放后卡在 `needs_evidence`（过渡不恢复）\n\nOrder in `add_evidence` (`correction_service.py:768-789`): append `correction.evidence.appended` event → `_transition` needs_evidence→submitted → save. The dedupe early-return (`759-760`) triggers as soon as the evidence record is in the event stream, **before** the transition. Crash between event append and transition → retry: `load()` rebuilds the record from the stream, early-returns, and the proposal stays in `needs_evidence` forever; the deterministic transition is never resumed. Note the asymmetry: `validate` does **not** have this bug — its record only enters the stream via the transition event itself, so a retry re-runs the transition (self-heals). `record_owner_decision` also handles its crash window correctly (replay re-runs the approval side effect, `920-930`). Only `add_evidence` is broken.\n\n**Remediation**: on the early-return path, if `state == NEEDS_EVIDENCE`, still attempt the deterministic `_transition` (idempotent at the executor level via request_id), or reorder so the transition is attempted before/independent of the dedupe return. Add a crash-window test (evidence event present, transition missing → retry must reach `submitted`).\n\n### Q1 — 自包含修订包没有生产者，服务模型与导出 Schema 词汇不对应（验收 6 只测了 Schema 层）\n\n`src/ci_workflow` contains **no export/producer** for the self-contained package (grep for 修订包/export confirms only error strings and the schema description). The service model and the schema do not map 1:1 anywhere: `report` vs `report_kind`; `snapshot_sha256` vs `snapshot_content_digest`; `rationale_zh` vs `user_reason_zh`; `source_records`/`CorrectionAttachment(label,url)` vs `evidence_items`/`EvidenceItem(evidence_id, source_url|source_version_id, description_zh)`; `conflicts: tuple[str]` vs `Conflict` objects; `impact_set: tuple[str]` vs structured `ImpactSet`; validation `outcome: passed` vs schema `disposition: validated_pending_user_approval`; `published_version` vs `published_report_version_id`. Acceptance #6 is therefore verified only by hand-built contract-test payloads.
